Three complete paired rounds measure the existing original Desktop automatic ceiling of 33 decimal GB on the owned 48-GB M5 Pro. Both arms receive the same explicit ceiling and unchanged complete workloads. The native and parent supervisors require at least 36 GB actual reclaimable memory. All six processes complete within physical ceilings, retain natural-answer completion, stay eligible under the previously frozen timing policy, and report zero whole-host swap-ins and swap-outs. Total campaign duration is 1,038.932 seconds. This is the actual original default ceiling; the research candidate's compiled default remains 22 GB and no product memory policy changes.

The binary is the exact CI artifact for source commit 754426d7915d26f007fed2c5ec53d633d0664596. Its archive checksum, binary identity, reconstructed source and live source agreement pass. Engine, Mac and context CI all pass for that exact head. The narrow diagnostic bound extends only prospective V2 pilots to the existing 33-GB ceiling; legacy and held-out bounds stay at 24 GB. Twenty-three Python runner checks pass. Both actual allocation proposals pass without loading a model before the complete run.

The original's generation medians exceed twenty committed tokens per second on all three workloads. The smaller attention candidate is slower on both fixed-length workloads and slightly faster on the natural coding answer; all three of its short-workload repetitions remain just below twenty. It improves first-text latency and the longer-prompt/coding request latencies while loading more slowly. The natural coding output has identical text and all 81 token IDs across all six processes. Three pilot rounds do not establish the instrument's statistical lower bound, universal throughput, sustained-session or other-Mac performance. No product promotion follows.

An allocation tradeoff is explicit in the complete plans: the original uses a 2,048-row prefill allowance while the candidate prices 4,096 rows. Their pool capacities are 7,509 and 8,800 expert records. The candidate reserves more prefill workspace despite every tested prompt fitting within 2,048 rows; any trade of that allowance for a larger cache must be a separate prospective comparison and must also acknowledge longer-prompt costs. Unused observed capacity alone is not permission to remove a reserve.

The identity-checked Photos analysis pause automatically resumes after the owned parent exits; its restoration receipt is included. The parent-bound caffeinate assertion ends and no model remains. The same two-minute continuously quiet nominal starts and all pressure, headroom, CPU, footprint and staging guards remain. Filesystem cache is uncontrolled. Exact small input/output files follow as zlib/base64 with original byte counts and SHA-256; no weight payload is embedded.
